# Windows — inferred

The **7,605** projects that probably run on Windows but do not say so. Each verdict here comes from the install route or the language runtime, never from a release asset or from the README — that is what puts them on this page rather than with the [3,334 confirmed ones](windows.md).

Read `Why` before trusting a row. A portable runtime is a good bet and not a promise.

[← Windows](windows.md) · [← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **10** of 11, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](windows-inferred.md) · [2](windows-inferred-2.md) · [3](windows-inferred-3.md) · [4](windows-inferred-4.md) · [5](windows-inferred-5.md) · [6](windows-inferred-6.md) · [7](windows-inferred-7.md) · [8](windows-inferred-8.md) · [9](windows-inferred-9.md) · **10** · [11](windows-inferred-11.md) — [continue on page 11 →](windows-inferred-11.md)

Snapshot 2026-11-28. Stars, language, licence and last-push come from the GitHub API and drift daily.

Filter the same data in the browser on the [Atlas site](https://aaa.jeremyfhall.com/catalog/). Spreadsheet edition: retired. The last copy, built 2026-10-22, stays available but is no longer updated. The website is the maintained edition. Its last workbooks: [dark](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-DARK.xlsx) · [light](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-LIGHT.xlsx).
